# Sources manifest (native 0.54.4)

The repo ShelaShelby/physics-lab is public. Published papers, preprints, journal scans, extracted paper text, saved web pages and downloads whose terms are unclear are NOT in it: they are kept in the private repo **ShelaShelby/physics-lab-sources** (branch `main`), at the same relative path under `data/` (`native/data/x/y.pdf` here is `data/x/y.pdf` there). This file lists every such file, one line each: its path in the sources repo, its full reference, URL, the date it was read, its sha256 and what uses it (tests, tools, CMake; code comments in src). Where a test needs numbers from a file, the file's extract is next to where the file was: `<name>.extract.txt` (reference, URL, date, sha256 and place in its header; the lines copied exactly).

Check it with `python3 native/tools/check_sources.py <checkout of the sources repo>` (or set `PL_SOURCES_DIR`): every line below (file exists, sha256 matches) and every extract (its text is found in the original). A session that saves or reads a source attaches the sources repo (add_repo, push access), pushes to `main`, adds a line here and runs the check. A number is still checked against the saved original before it is used.

Kept in this repo, not listed here: the project's own outputs (calculations, generated tables, DFT files), small tables typed from a source with the reference beside them, and raw datasets of US government sources (NIST, NASA/JPL, NASA PDS; listed per folder in each README.txt). The BSD-3-Clause source files of colour-science 0.4.7 also stay (`data/colour-science_v0.4.7_LICENSE.txt`).

Dates are the date the file was read as the folder README or its manifest records it; "(date first committed)" or "(not recorded)" says when the README does not give one.
